Remarks.&mdash;During January the highest reading of the barometer was 30.477, on the 15th; and the lowest 30.062, on the 30th. The highest temperature was 8o&deg;, on the 12th, 23rd, and 27th; and the lowest 43&deg;, on the 15th. N.E. winds prevailed. Rain fell on 3 days. During February the highest reading of the barometer was 30.690, on the 12th; and the lowest 29.790, on the 4th. The highest temperature was 82&deg;, on the 24th and 27th; and the lowest 41&deg;, on the 6th. 8.E. winds prevailed. Rain fell on 6 days. During March the highest reading of the barometer was 30.460, on the 23rd; and the lowest 29.820, on the 16th. The highest temperature was 76&deg;.5, on the 1st; and the lowest 440, on the 27th. N.E. winds prevailed. Rain fell on 19 days. IX.
